# SearchTern

### The all-in-one internship platform for college students

[![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Visit-SearchTern.com-1D9E75?style=for-the-badge)](https://searchtern.ksaif.dev/)
[![Listings](https://img.shields.io/badge/Internships-19,810-blue?style=for-the-badge)](https://github.com/KSaifStack/jobscraper)
[![Updated](https://img.shields.io/badge/Updated-November%2012,%202026-orange?style=for-the-badge)](https://github.com/KSaifStack/jobscraper)

</div>

---

> 🎓 **19,810 internships & new grad roles** refreshed every 4 hours from 45 ATS platforms and community job boards worldwide.
> Finding an internship has never been harder — students are sending 500–1000+ applications just to land one.
> SearchTern breaks that cycle. [**Start your search →**](https://searchtern.ksaif.dev/)

### 🛠️ How it works

SearchTern merges three feeds into one deduplicated list:

1. **ATS scrapers** — [**jobhive**](https://github.com/stapply-ai/ats-scrapers) by [Stapply](https://data.stapply.ai)
   pulls roles straight from 45 ATS platforms (Greenhouse, Lever, Ashby, Workday & 41 more) where
   companies actually post. No LinkedIn reposts. No duplicates.
2. **Board probes** — our own scraper hits big-tech boards jobhive misses (Workday CXS sites,
   SmartRecruiters, and a handful of direct Greenhouse/Lever/Ashby boards).
3. **Community feeds** — Simplify / speedyapply / zapply internship lists, the lilguy live feed,
   SkillExchange, EchoJobs, plus Indeed, ZipRecruiter & Google results via JobSpy.

Every row is filtered to internships & new-grad roles, deduplicated, and pruned to the last 60 days.
The light tier refreshes hourly; the full run every 4 hours.

The **H-1B** column comes from federal filing data: `role` means the company filed H-1B
labor-condition applications for this job title, `company` means it sponsors H-1B but not for
this exact title, and blank means no signal. Full employer and job-title data lives in
[pages/h1b.json](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/KSaifStack/SearchTern-Listings/main/pages/h1b.json).

**Machine-readable feeds:** [full feed](https://github.com/KSaifStack/SearchTern-Listings/releases/download/feed-latest/searchtern.json) ·
[15-minute fast lane](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/KSaifStack/SearchTern-Listings/main/pages/priority.json) ·
[H-1B sponsorship data](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/KSaifStack/SearchTern-Listings/main/pages/h1b.json) ·
[RSS (newest 200)](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/KSaifStack/SearchTern-Listings/main/pages/feed.xml).
Agents can query the same data through the bundled MCP server (`Listings/searchtern_mcp.py`:
`search_jobs`, `recent_jobs`, `company_jobs`, `stats`).

> jobhive does the heavy lifting — if you find it useful, ⭐ [stapply-ai/ats-scrapers](https://github.com/stapply-ai/ats-scrapers)
